# Project Overview

## 1. Project Summary
SentinelAI is an AI-powered mini SOC platform for security analysts, admins, and viewers. It ingests security events, normalizes and detects suspicious activity, correlates alerts into incidents/offenses, scores risk, provides dashboards and investigation pages, and optionally enriches investigation with local/LLM AI plus a Python ML scoring service.

End-to-end request flow found in code:
1. React UI in `frontend/src` calls Axios services in `frontend/src/services`.
2. `frontend/src/services/api.js` prefixes calls with `/api`, attaches JWT access tokens, and refreshes expired sessions through `/auth/refresh`.
3. Spring controllers in `backend/src/main/java/com/sentinelai/**/web` receive `/api/**` requests.
4. Controllers call services such as ingestion, detection, incident, dashboard, AI, playbook, simulator, site, and admin-risk services.
5. Services use Spring Data repositories and Flyway-managed MySQL tables. Kafka and Redis are used when enabled; otherwise code contains fallback paths.
6. ML integration is through the backend ML client and Python FastAPI service at `ML_BASE_URL` (`http://localhost:8000` by default), posting numeric feature vectors to `/score`.
7. Responses return JSON DTOs to Axios, then React pages render tables, dashboards, panels, charts, and Three.js visuals.

## 2. Tech Stack
### Backend
- Java `21`; Spring Boot `3.3.5`; Maven.
- Spring Web, Security, Data JPA, Validation, Actuator, Kafka, Redis/Lettuce.
- Flyway, MySQL Connector/J, Lombok, springdoc OpenAPI `2.6.0`, JJWT `0.12.6`.
- Testcontainers `1.20.4`, JaCoCo `0.8.12`, OWASP Dependency Check `10.0.4`.

### Frontend
- React `^18.3.1`, Vite `^5.4.10`, React Router DOM `^6.27.0`, Axios `^1.7.7`, Recharts `^2.15.4`, Framer Motion `^11.18.2`, Three `^0.160.1`, @react-three/fiber `^8.17.10`, @react-three/drei `^9.114.0`, Playwright `^1.63.0`, Vitest `^2.1.9`.

### ML
- fastapi==0.111.0
- uvicorn[standard]==0.30.1
- scikit-learn==1.5.1
- pandas==2.2.2
- numpy==1.26.4
- joblib==1.4.2
- pydantic==2.8.2
- requests==2.32.3
- pytest==8.2.2
- httpx==0.27.0
- shap==0.46.0

### Infrastructure and Tools
MySQL 8, Redis, Kafka KRaft, Caddy, optional Cloudflare Tunnel, Prometheus, Grafana, k6, Sonar, Gitleaks, Trivy reports, GitHub Actions.

## 3. Folder Structure
```text
sentinel-ai/
├── .github/                 CI/CD workflows and modernization hooks.
├── .vscode/                 Workspace editor settings.
├── agent/                   Python helper/agent entrypoint.
├── backend/                 Spring Boot API, domain, services, migrations, tests, Dockerfile.
├── backups/                 Database backup artifacts.
├── docs/                    Project documentation, ADRs, screenshots, reports, security notes.
├── frontend/                React/Vite UI, services, components, E2E tests, Docker/nginx config.
├── infrastructure/          Docker Compose, Caddy, Grafana, Prometheus, Kubernetes placeholder.
├── load-tests/              k6 scripts.
├── ml/                      FastAPI/scikit-learn ML training, inference, models, tests.
├── reports/                 Security scan outputs.
└── scripts/                 Demo, deploy, rollback, backup, restore, benchmark, scan scripts.
```

Routing: `frontend/src/App.jsx` defines React Router routes and protected pages. Auth state lives in `frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx`. Tokens are stored by `frontend/src/services/tokenStore.js`; API interception and refresh handling are in `frontend/src/services/api.js`. Styling is centralized in `frontend/src/styles.css`, `frontend/src/theme/tokens.css`, and `frontend/src/theme/ui.css`.

Frontend service API calls:
- `frontend/src/services/admin.service.js` calls `GET '/admin/pending'`
- `frontend/src/services/admin.service.js` calls `POST `/admin/pending/${id}/approve``
- `frontend/src/services/admin.service.js` calls `POST `/admin/pending/${id}/reject``
- `frontend/src/services/admin.service.js` calls `GET '/admin/timeline', { params }`

- `frontend/src/services/admin.service.js` calls `POST `/admin/sessions/${userId}/revoke``

## 6. Configuration
- `backend/src/main/resources/application.yml`: port `8080`, JWT TTLs, CORS defaults, request-size limits, ML, Redis, Kafka, AI, baseline, graph, detection, Actuator/Prometheus.
- `backend/src/main/resources/application-dev.yml`: MySQL `localhost:3306/sentinelai`, user/password `sentinel`, Flyway enabled, dev JWT default, simulator/demo on, Redis/Kafka on by default, AI provider `local`.
- `backend/src/main/resources/application-demo.yml`: self-contained demo, CORS `http://localhost:8088,http://localhost:5173`, Redis/Kafka off, local AI.
- `backend/src/main/resources/application-prod.yml`: env-provided DB credentials, SSL DB URL, HSTS on.
- `infrastructure/docker/.env.example`: `IMAGE_BASE`, `TAG`, `SITE_ADDRESS`, `PUBLIC_URL`, `DB_*`, `JWT_SECRET`, `ADMIN_*`, `KAFKA_ENABLED`, `AI_ENABLED`, `AI_PROVIDER`, `LLM_API_KEY`, `TUNNEL_TOKEN`.
- Dockerfiles exist for backend, frontend, and ML. CI files: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`.

## 7. Integration
Frontend calls backend through Axios `/api`. Backend uses JDBC/JPA/Flyway for MySQL, Redis for cache/rate/window storage, Kafka for event pipeline, ML HTTP to `ML_BASE_URL`, and local/fake/http AI clients for investigation.

## 8. How To Run
```bash
docker compose -f infrastructure/docker/docker-compose.yml up -d
cd backend && ./mvnw spring-boot:run
cd frontend && npm install && npm run dev
cd ml && python3.11 -m venv .venv && . .venv/bin/activate && pip install -r requirements.txt && python train.py && uvicorn app:app --port 8000
./scripts/demo.sh up
```
Backend: `http://localhost:8080`; frontend: `http://localhost:5173`; demo: `http://localhost:8088`; ML: `http://localhost:8000`.

## 10. Known Issues, TODOs, Unused Files, Security Concerns
- Dev/demo profiles contain default JWT secrets and public demo credentials; code comments mark these as non-production.
- `infrastructure/docker/.env` exists; contents were not copied here. Security concern: real secrets should remain uncommitted and rotated if exposed.
- Generated folders/artifacts exist: `frontend/node_modules`, `frontend/dist`, `backend/target`, `ml/.venv`, `ml/__pycache__`, `.pytest_cache`.
- `backups/sentinelai-20261006-132456.sql.gz` exists; backup contents were not analyzed.
- Some endpoint request/response shapes are DTO-specific and not fully reconstructable from route annotations alone; where not obvious, this document says Not found in code.
